---@param cb function Callback function to execute safely
---@param ... any Arguments to pass to callback
---@return any|nil result Returns callback result or nil on error
function qbLib.safecall(cb, ...)
    assert(type(cb) == 'function', 'safecall: callback must be a function')

    local success, result = pcall(cb, ...)

    if not success then
        local info = debug.getinfo(2, 'Sl')
        local source = info.source:match('^@?(.+)$') or info.source
        local line = info.currentline or 0

        lib.print.error(('safecall failed at %s:%d\n%s'):format(source, line, result))
        return nil
    end

    return result
end

---Returns the given string with its trailing whitespaces removed.
---@param str string
---@return string
function qbLib.string.trim(str)
    local trimmed = str:gsub('^%s*(.-)%s*$', '%1')
    return trimmed
end

---Returns true if the given string is nil, empty, or contains only whitespace.
---@param str string?
---@return boolean
function qbLib.string.isEmpty(str)
    return str == nil or str:match('^%s*$') ~= nil
end

---Returns the given string with its first character capitalized.
---@param str string
---@return string
function qbLib.string.capitalize(str)
    local capitalized = str:gsub('^%l', string.upper)
    return capitalized
end

---Returns the given string with its first character lowercased (camelCase start).
---@param str string
---@return string
function qbLib.string.uncapitalize(str)
    ---@diagnostic disable-next-line: redundant-return-value
    return str:gsub('^%u', string.lower)
end

---Rounds and returns the given number.
---@param num number
---@param decimalPlaces? integer
---@return number rounded integer if `decimalPlaces` isn't passed, number otherwise
function qbLib.math.round(num, decimalPlaces)
    if not decimalPlaces then return math.floor(num + 0.5) end
    local power = 10 ^ decimalPlaces
    return math.floor((num * power) + 0.5) / power
end

---@param t table<string, any>
---@param fn fun(val, key):any
function qbLib.table.find(t, fn)
    for k, v in pairs(t) do
        local f = fn and fn(v, k)

        if f then
            return v, k
        end
    end
end

---Returns a shallow copy of the given table (nested tables are not cloned).
---@param tbl table
---@return table
function qbLib.table.clone(tbl)
    local copy = {}
    for k, v in pairs(tbl) do
        copy[k] = v
    end
    return copy
end

---Returns a deep copy of the given table (all nested tables are recursively cloned).
---@param tbl table
---@return table
function qbLib.table.deepclone(tbl)
    local copy = {}
    for k, v in pairs(tbl) do
        if type(v) == 'table' then
            copy[k] = qbLib.table.deepclone(v)
        else
            copy[k] = v
        end
    end
    return copy
end

---Returns the number of items in a table. Useful for non-array tables.
---@param tbl table
---@return integer
function qbLib.table.size(tbl)
    local size = 0
    for _ in pairs(tbl) do
        size += 1
    end
    return size
end

---Maps and returns the values of the given table by the given subfield.
---@param tble table
---@param subfield any
---@return table<any, table[]>
function qbLib.table.mapBySubfield(tble, subfield)
    local map = {}

    for _, subTable in pairs(tble) do
        local subfieldValue = subTable[subfield]

        if subfieldValue then
            if not map[subfieldValue] then
                map[subfieldValue] = {}
            end

            map[subfieldValue][#map[subfieldValue] + 1] = subTable
        end
    end

    return map
end

---Returns the number plate of the given vehicle.
---@param vehicle integer
---@return string?
function qbLib.getVehiclePlate(vehicle)
    local plate = GetVehicleNumberPlateText(vehicle)
    if not plate then return end
    return qbLib.string.trim(plate)
end

---Generates and returns a random number plate with the given pattern.
---Note that the generated plate may or may not be already used by an existing vehicle.
---For more info about the pattern see [`lib.string.random`](https://coxdocs.dev/ox_lib/Modules/String/Shared#stringrandom) from ox_lib.
---@param pattern? string
---@return string
function qbLib.generateRandomPlate(pattern)
    pattern = pattern or '........'
    return lib.string.random(pattern):upper()
end

---Returns the cardinal direction that the given entity is staring towards, or nil if the entity doesn't exist.
---```
---                 North
---     45Â°           0Â°           315Â°
---       \    .- - - - - - -.    /
---          X                 X
---        .'   \           /   '.
---       |        \     /        |
--- West  |           X           |  East
---       |        /     \        |
---        '.   /           \   .'
---          X                 X
---       /    '- - - - - - -'    \
---     135Â°                      225Â°
---                 South
---```
---(art inspired by [`SET_PED_TURNING_THRESHOLDS`](https://github.com/citizenfx/fivem/blob/0b61c93a3308360ea70d443398eab17f47453e11/code/components/extra-natives-five/src/PedExtraNatives.cpp#L390-L400))
---@param entity integer
---@return 'North' | 'South' | 'East' | 'West'
function qbLib.getCardinalDirection(entity)
    -- heading is between 0 - 360 (excluding 360)
    local heading = GetEntityHeading(entity) % 360

    if heading < 45 or heading >= 315 then
        return 'North'
    end

    if heading >= 45 and heading < 135 then
        return 'West'
    end

    if heading >= 135 and heading < 225 then
        return 'South'
    end

    -- heading >= 225 and heading < 315
    return 'East'
end

---@param playerGroups PlayerJob
---@param job_table string|string[]|table<string, number|number[]>
---@param required_duty? boolean
---@return boolean
function qbLib.checkGroup(playerGroups, job_table, required_duty)
    if not playerGroups then return false end

    local jn = playerGroups.name
    local jt = playerGroups.type
    local jd = playerGroups.onduty
    local jg = playerGroups.grade.level

    if required_duty and not jd then
        return false
    end

    local tt = type(job_table)

    if tt == 'string' then
        return jn == job_table or jt == job_table
    end

    if tt == 'table' then
        if lib.array.isArray(job_table) then
            return lib.table.contains(job_table, jn) or lib.table.contains(job_table, jt)
        end

        local requirement = job_table[jn] or job_table[jt]
        if not requirement then return false end

        local rt = type(requirement)

        if rt == 'number' then
            return jg >= requirement
        end

        if rt == 'table' and lib.array.isArray(requirement) then
            return lib.table.contains(requirement, jg)
        end

        return false
    end

    return false
end

if isServer then
    ---@class LibSpawnVehicleParams
    ---@field model integer
    ---@field spawnSource integer | vector3 | vector4 ped id or coords
    ---@field warp? boolean | integer a ped id to warp inside the vehicle, or additionally a boolean if `spawnSource` is a ped
    ---@field props? table https://coxdocs.dev/ox_lib/Modules/VehicleProperties/Client#vehicle-properties
    ---@field bucket? integer routing bucket to move spawned entity to.

    ---Creates a vehicle on the server-side and returns its `netId`.
    ---
    ---The `CreateVehicleServerSetter` native uses only the server to create a vehicle instead of using the client as well.
    ---To get the vehicle on the client first check and wait for it to exist using the `NetworkDoesEntityExistWithNetworkId` native,
    ---then get the vehicle's id by using `NetToVeh`.
    --[[

    lib.waitFor(function()
        if NetworkDoesEntityExistWithNetworkId(netId) then
            return NetToVeh(netId)
        end
    end)
    ]]
    ---@param params LibSpawnVehicleParams
    ---@return integer netId
    ---@return number veh
    function qbLib.spawnVehicle(params)
        local model = params.model
        local source = params.spawnSource
        local sourceType = type(source)
        local warp = params.warp
        local ped = type(warp) == 'number' and warp or (sourceType == 'number' and warp and source or nil)
        local props = params.props
        local bucket = params.bucket or ped and GetEntityRoutingBucket(ped) or nil

        ---@type vector4
        local coords
        if sourceType == 'vector3' then
            coords = vec4(source.x, source.y, source.z, 0)
        elseif sourceType == 'vector4' then
            coords = source
        else
            local pedCoords = GetEntityCoords(source)
            coords = vec4(pedCoords.x, pedCoords.y, pedCoords.z, GetEntityHeading(source))
        end

        local vehData = exports.sm_core:GetVehiclesByHash(joaat(model))
        local vehicleType = vehData and vehData.type or nil

        if not vehicleType then
            warn(('No vehicle type found for model: %s temp vehicle was created and taken in type'):format(model))
            local tempVehicle = CreateVehicle(model, 0, 0, -200, 0, true, true)
            while not DoesEntityExist(tempVehicle) do Wait(0) end
            vehicleType = GetVehicleType(tempVehicle)
            DeleteEntity(tempVehicle)
        end

        local attempts = 0

        local veh, netId
        while attempts < 3 do
            veh = CreateVehicleServerSetter(model, vehicleType, coords.x, coords.y, coords.z, coords.w)

            if bucket and bucket > 0 then
                exports.sm_core:SetEntityBucket(veh, bucket)
            end

            while not DoesEntityExist(veh) do Wait(0) end
            while GetVehicleNumberPlateText(veh) == '' do Wait(0) end

            if ped then
                SetPedIntoVehicle(ped, veh, -1)
            end

            if not pcall(function()
                lib.waitFor(function()
                    local owner = NetworkGetEntityOwner(veh)
                    if ped then
                        --- the owner should be transferred to the driver
                        if owner == NetworkGetEntityOwner(ped) then return true end
                    else
                        if owner ~= -1 then return true end
                    end
                end, 'client never set as owner', 15000)
            end) then
                DeleteEntity(veh)
                error('Deleting vehicle which timed out finding an owner')
            end

            local state = Entity(veh).state
            local owner = NetworkGetEntityOwner(veh)
            state:set('initVehicle', true, true)
            netId = NetworkGetNetworkIdFromEntity(veh)
            if props and type(props) == 'table' and props.plate then
                TriggerClientEvent('sm_core:client:setVehicleProperties', owner, netId, props)
                local success = pcall(function()
                    local plateMatched = false
                    lib.waitFor(function()
                        if qbLib.string.trim(GetVehicleNumberPlateText(veh)) == qbLib.string.trim(props.plate) then
                            local currentOwner = NetworkGetEntityOwner(veh)
                            assert(currentOwner == owner, ('Owner changed during vehicle init. expected=%s, actual=%s'):format(owner, currentOwner))
                            --- check that the plate matches twice, 100ms apart as a bug has been observed in which server side matches but plate is not observed by clients to match
                            if plateMatched then
                                return true
                            end
                            plateMatched = true
                            Wait(100)
                        end
                    end, 'Failed to set vehicle properties within 5 second', 5000)
                end)
                if success then
                    break
                else
                    DeleteEntity(veh)
                    attempts += 1
                end
            else
                break
            end
        end

        if attempts == 3 then
            error('unable to successfully spawn vehicle after 3 attempts')
        end

        --- prevent server from deleting a vehicle without an owner
        SetEntityOrphanMode(veh, 2)
        return netId, veh
    end
else
    ---@class LibDrawTextParams
    ---@field text string
    ---@field scale? integer default: `0.35`
    ---@field font? integer default: `4`
    ---@field color? vector4 rgba, white by default
    ---@field enableDropShadow? boolean
    ---@field enableOutline? boolean

    ---@class LibDrawText2DParams : LibDrawTextParams
    ---@field coords vector2
    ---@field width? number default: `1.0`
    ---@field height? number default: `1.0`

    ---Draws text onto the screen in 2D space for a single frame.
    ---@param params LibDrawText2DParams
    function qbLib.drawText2d(params)
        local text = params.text
        local coords = params.coords
        local scale = params.scale or 0.35
        local font = params.font or 4
        local color = params.color or vec4(255, 255, 255, 255)
        local width = params.width or 1.0
        local height = params.height or 1.0
        local enableDropShadow = params.enableDropShadow or false
        local enableOutline = params.enableOutline or false

        SetTextScale(scale, scale)
        SetTextFont(font)
        SetTextColour(math.floor(color.r), math.floor(color.g), math.floor(color.b), math.floor(color.a))
        if enableDropShadow then
            SetTextDropShadow()
        end
        if enableOutline then
            SetTextOutline()
        end

        SetTextCentre(true)
        BeginTextCommandDisplayText('STRING')
        AddTextComponentSubstringPlayerName(text)
        EndTextCommandDisplayText(coords.x - width / 2, coords.y - height / 2 + 0.005)
    end

    ---@class LibDrawText3DParams : LibDrawTextParams
    ---@field coords vector3
    ---@field disableDrawRect? boolean
    ---@field scale? integer | vector2 default: `vec2(0.35,0.35)`

    ---Draws text onto the screen in 3D space for a single frame.
    ---@param params LibDrawText3DParams
    function qbLib.drawText3d(params) -- luacheck: ignore
        local isScaleparamANumber = type(params.scale) == "number"
        local text = params.text
        local coords = params.coords

        ---@diagnostic disable-next-line: param-type-mismatch
        local scale = (isScaleparamANumber and vec2(params.scale, params.scale))
        or params.scale
        or vec2(0.35, 0.35)

        local font = params.font or 4
        local color = params.color or vec4(255, 255, 255, 255)
        local enableDropShadow = params.enableDropShadow or false
        local enableOutline = params.enableOutline or false

        SetTextScale(scale.x, scale.y)
        SetTextFont(font)
        SetTextColour(math.floor(color.r), math.floor(color.g), math.floor(color.b), math.floor(color.a))
        if enableDropShadow then
            SetTextDropShadow()
        end
        if enableOutline then
            SetTextOutline()
        end
        SetTextCentre(true)
        BeginTextCommandDisplayText('STRING')
        AddTextComponentSubstringPlayerName(text)
        SetDrawOrigin(coords.x, coords.y, coords.z, 0)
        EndTextCommandDisplayText(0.0, 0.0)

        if not params.disableDrawRect then
            local factor = #text / 370
            DrawRect(0.0, 0.0125, 0.017 + factor, 0.03, 0, 0, 0, 75)
        end
        ClearDrawOrigin()
    end

    ---Gets and returns an entity handle and network id from a state bag name
    ---([source](https://github.com/overextended/ox_core/blob/main/client/utils.lua)).
    ---Statebag handlers receive changes for every entity on the server, including
    ---entities outside this client's scope which will never exist locally. Those
    ---must return quietly instead of throwing, so the wait is short and guarded.
    ---@async
    ---@param bagName string
    ---@return integer? entity, integer? netId
    function qbLib.getEntityAndNetIdFromBagName(bagName)
        local netId = tonumber(bagName:gsub('entity:', ''), 10)
        if not netId then return end

        if NetworkDoesEntityExistWithNetworkId(netId) then
            return NetworkGetEntityFromNetworkId(netId), netId
        end

        local ok, entity = pcall(lib.waitFor, function()
            if NetworkDoesEntityExistWithNetworkId(netId) then
                return NetworkGetEntityFromNetworkId(netId)
            end
        end, nil, 1500)

        if not ok or not entity or entity == 0 then return end

        return entity, netId
    end

    ---Returns a state bag handler made for entities
    ---([source](https://github.com/overextended/ox_core/blob/main/client/utils.lua)).
    ---@param keyFilter string
    ---@param cb fun(entity: number, netId: number, value: any, bagName: string)
    ---@return number
    function qbLib.entityStateHandler(keyFilter, cb)
        return AddStateBagChangeHandler(keyFilter, '', function(bagName, _, value)
            local entity, netId = qbLib.getEntityAndNetIdFromBagName(bagName)
            if not entity or entity == 0 or not netId then return end

            cb(entity, netId, value, bagName)
        end)
    end

    ---Deletes the specified vehicle and returns whether it was successful.
    ---@param vehicle integer
    ---@return boolean deleted
    function qbLib.deleteVehicle(vehicle)
        SetEntityAsMissionEntity(vehicle, true, true)
        DeleteVehicle(vehicle)
        return not DoesEntityExist(vehicle)
    end

    ---Returns the model name of the given vehicle.
    ---@param vehicle integer
    ---@return string
    function qbLib.getVehicleDisplayName(vehicle)
        return GetLabelText(GetDisplayNameFromVehicleModel(GetEntityModel(vehicle)))
    end

    ---Returns the brand name of the given vehicle.
    ---@param vehicle integer
    ---@return string
    function qbLib.getVehicleMakeName(vehicle)
        return GetLabelText(GetMakeNameFromVehicleModel(GetEntityModel(vehicle)))
    end

    ---Returns the mod type name of the given vehicle.
    ---@param vehicle integer
    ---@param modType integer
    ---@param modIndex integer
    ---@return string
    function qbLib.getVehicleModName(vehicle, modType, modIndex)
        return GetLabelText(GetModTextLabel(vehicle, modType, modIndex))
    end

    ---Returns the livery name of the given vehicle.
    ---@param vehicle integer
    ---@param liveryIndex integer
    ---@return string
    function qbLib.getVehicleLiveryName(vehicle, liveryIndex)
        return GetLabelText(GetLiveryName(vehicle, liveryIndex))
    end

    ---Returns the street name and cross section name at the given coords.
    ---@param coords vector3
    ---@return { main: string, cross: string }
    function qbLib.getStreetName(coords)
        local street1, street2 = GetStreetNameAtCoord(coords.x, coords.y, coords.z)
        return { main = GetStreetNameFromHashKey(street1), cross = GetStreetNameFromHashKey(street2) }
    end

    ---Returns the name of the zone at the given coords.
    ---@param coords vector3
    ---@return string
    function qbLib.getZoneName(coords)
        return GetLabelText(GetNameOfZone(coords.x, coords.y, coords.z))
    end

    ---Set an extra on the given vehicle.
    ---@param vehicle integer
    ---@param extra integer
    ---@param enable boolean
    function qbLib.setVehicleExtra(vehicle, extra, enable)
        if not DoesExtraExist(vehicle, extra) then return end
        SetVehicleExtra(vehicle, extra, not enable)
    end

    ---Enables all the extras of the given vehicle.
    ---@param vehicle integer
    function qbLib.resetVehicleExtras(vehicle)
        for i = 1, 20 do
            qbLib.setVehicleExtra(vehicle, i, true)
        end
    end

    ---Sets all the extras of the given vehicle.
    ---@param vehicle integer
    ---@param extras table<integer, boolean>
    function qbLib.setVehicleExtras(vehicle, extras)
        qbLib.resetVehicleExtras(vehicle)

        for id, enable in pairs(extras) do
            qbLib.setVehicleExtra(vehicle, id, enable)
        end
    end

    ---Returns if the local ped is wearing gloves.
    ---@return boolean
    function qbLib.isWearingGloves()
        local armIndex = GetPedDrawableVariation(cache.ped, 3)
        local model = GetEntityModel(cache.ped)
        local tble = qbLib.armsWithoutGloves[model == `mp_m_freemode_01` and 'male' or 'female']
        return not tble[armIndex]
    end

    --- Returns if the local ped is wearing a duffel bag.
    --- @return boolean
    function qbLib.isWearingDuffelbag()
        local torsoIndex = GetPedDrawableVariation(cache.ped, 5) -- Duffel bags are in component 5
        local model = GetEntityModel(cache.ped)
        local tble = qbLib.duffelbagIndexes[model == `mp_m_freemode_01` and 'male' or 'female']
        return tble[torsoIndex] == true
    end

    ---Attempts to load an audio bank and returns whether it was successful.
    ---Remember to use `ReleaseScriptAudioBank` since you can only load up to 10 banks.
    ---@param audioBank string
    ---@param timeout number?
    ---@return boolean
    function qbLib.loadAudioBank(audioBank, timeout)
        return lib.waitFor(function()
            if RequestScriptAudioBank(audioBank, false) then
                return true
            end
        end, ('timed out while requesting audio bank! (%s)'):format(audioBank), timeout or 500) or false
    end

    ---@class LibPlayAudioParams
    ---@field audioName string
    ---@field audioRef string
    ---@field returnSoundId? boolean
    ---@field audioSource? number | vector3 entity handle or vector3 coords
    ---@field range? number only used if `audioSource` is a vector3 coordinate

    ---@deprecated use mana_audio instead
    ---Plays a sound with the provided audio name and audio ref.
    ---If `returnSoundId` is false or not specified the soundId is released,
    ---otherwise the function returns the soundId without releasing it.
    ---@param params LibPlayAudioParams
    ---@return number? soundId
    function qbLib.playAudio(params)
        local audioName = params.audioName
        local audioRef = params.audioRef
        local returnSoundId = params.returnSoundId or false
        local source = params.audioSource
        local range = params.range or 5.0

        local soundId = GetSoundId()

        local sourceType = type(source)
        if sourceType == 'vector3' then
            local coords = source
            PlaySoundFromCoord(soundId, audioName, coords.x, coords.y, coords.z, audioRef, false, range, false)
        elseif sourceType == 'number' then
            PlaySoundFromEntity(soundId, audioName, source, audioRef, false, false)
        else
            PlaySoundFrontend(soundId, audioName, audioRef, true)
        end

        if returnSoundId then
            return soundId
        end

        ReleaseSoundId(soundId)
    end
end

_ENV.qbLib = qbLib

########## FILE: \modules\playerdata.lua
---@meta

if lib.context == 'server' then return end

local sm_core = exports.sm_core

local data = sm_core:GetPlayerData() or {}
local proxies = setmetatable({}, { __mode = 'k' })

local readonly

local function wrap(value)
    return type(value) == 'table' and readonly(value) or value
end

---@generic T : table
---@param source T
---@return T
function readonly(source)
    local proxy = proxies[source]
    if proxy then return proxy end

    proxy = setmetatable({}, {
        __index = function(_, key) return wrap(source[key]) end,
        __newindex = function(_, key)
            error(('cannot set values on a read-only table (%s)'):format(tostring(key)), 2)
        end,
        __len = function() return #source end,
        __pairs = function()
            return function(_, key)
                local k, v = next(source, key)
                return k, wrap(v)
            end, source, nil
        end,
        __metatable = 'readonly',
    })

    proxies[source] = proxy

    return proxy
end

QBPlayerData = readonly(data)

local function setData(payload)
    table.wipe(data)

    for key, value in pairs(payload) do
        data[key] = value
    end
end

AddEventHandler('QBCore:Client:OnPlayerLoaded', function()
    setData(sm_core:GetPlayerData() or {})
end)

sm_core:registerHook('core:updatePlayerDataBykey', function(key, value, subValue)
    if key == 'all' then
        setData(value or {})
        return
    end

    if subValue == nil then
        data[key] = value
        return
    end

    local field = data[key]

    if type(field) ~= 'table' then
        field = {}
        data[key] = field
    end

    field[value] = subValue
end)

########## FILE: \modules\blip\client.lua
local functions = {}

---@class BlipOptions
---@field coords vector3|vector4 World coordinates for the blip
---@field sprite? integer Blip sprite ID (default: 1)
---@field color? integer Blip color ID (default: 0)
---@field scale? number Blip scale (default: 0.8)
---@field label? string Blip name shown on map (default: 'Blip')
---@field alpha? integer Blip opacity 0-255 (default: 255)
---@field shortRange? boolean Only show when nearby (default: false)
---@field display? integer Blip display type (default: 4)
---@field category? integer Blip category (default: 0)
---@field priority? integer Blip priority (default: 0)
---@field flashes? boolean Whether blip flashes (default: false)
---@field flashTimer? number Duration in ms before flash stops (0 = infinite)

---@class RadiusBlipOptions
---@field coords vector3|vector4 World coordinates for the radius blip center
---@field radius number Radius in units
---@field color? integer Blip color ID (default: 2)
---@field alpha? integer Blip opacity 0-255 (default: 80)
---@field label? string Blip name shown on map

---@class BlipEntry
---@field handle integer Native blip handle
---@field type 'coords'|'radius'
---@field id string Unique identifier

local blips = {}
local idCounter = 0

local function generateId()
    idCounter += 1
    return ('blip_%d_%d'):format(GetGameTimer(), idCounter)
end

-- â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
-- Internal helpers
-- â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€

local function applyBlipOptions(handle, opts)
    SetBlipSprite(handle, opts.sprite or 1)
    SetBlipColour(handle, opts.color or 0)
    SetBlipScale(handle, opts.scale or 0.8)
    SetBlipAlpha(handle, opts.alpha or 255)
    SetBlipDisplay(handle, opts.display or 4)
    SetBlipCategory(handle, opts.category or 0)
    SetBlipPriority(handle, opts.priority or 0)
    SetBlipAsShortRange(handle, opts.shortRange or false)

    local label = opts.label or 'Blip'
    BeginTextCommandSetBlipName('STRING')
    AddTextComponentSubstringPlayerName(label)
    EndTextCommandSetBlipName(handle)

    if opts.flashes then
        SetBlipFlashes(handle, true)
        if opts.flashTimer and opts.flashTimer > 0 then
            SetBlipFlashTimer(handle, opts.flashTimer)
        end
    end
end

-- â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
-- Public API
-- â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€

---Create a blip at specific world coordinates.
---@param opts BlipOptions
---@return string id  Unique blip ID for later reference
function functions.Add(opts)
    assert(opts and opts.coords, '[sm_core:blip] opts.coords is required')

    local coords = opts.coords
    local handle = AddBlipForCoord(coords.x, coords.y, coords.z)
    applyBlipOptions(handle, opts)

    local id = generateId()
    blips[id] = { handle = handle, type = 'coords', id = id }
    return id
end

---Create a radius (circle) blip at specific world coordinates.
---@param opts RadiusBlipOptions
---@return string id  Unique blip ID for later reference
function functions.AddRadius(opts)
    assert(opts and opts.coords, '[sm_core:blip] opts.coords is required')
    assert(opts.radius and opts.radius > 0, '[sm_core:blip] opts.radius must be > 0')

    local coords = opts.coords
    local handle = AddBlipForRadius(coords.x, coords.y, coords.z, opts.radius)

    SetBlipColour(handle, opts.color or 2)
    SetBlipAlpha(handle, opts.alpha or 80)

    if opts.label then
        BeginTextCommandSetBlipName('STRING')
        AddTextComponentSubstringPlayerName(opts.label)
        EndTextCommandSetBlipName(handle)
    end

    local id = generateId()
    blips[id] = { handle = handle, type = 'radius', id = id }
    return id
end

---Create a blip attached to an entity (vehicle, ped, object).
---@param entity integer Entity handle
---@param opts BlipOptions
---@return string id
function functions.AddForEntity(entity, opts)
    assert(entity and DoesEntityExist(entity), '[sm_core:blip] entity does not exist')

    local handle = AddBlipForEntity(entity)
    applyBlipOptions(handle, opts or {})

    local id = generateId()
    blips[id] = { handle = handle, type = 'entity', id = id }
    return id
end

---Remove a blip by its ID.
---@param id string
function functions.Remove(id)
    local entry = blips[id]
    if not entry then return end

    if DoesBlipExist(entry.handle) then
        RemoveBlip(entry.handle)
    end

    blips[id] = nil
end

---Remove all blips managed by this module.
function functions.RemoveAll()
    for id, entry in pairs(blips) do
        if DoesBlipExist(entry.handle) then
            RemoveBlip(entry.handle)
        end
        blips[id] = nil
    end
end

---Update the coords of an existing coord blip.
---@param id string
---@param coords vector3|vector4
function functions.SetCoords(id, coords)
    local entry = blips[id]
    if not entry or not DoesBlipExist(entry.handle) then return end
    SetBlipCoords(entry.handle, coords.x, coords.y, coords.z)
end

---Update the sprite of an existing blip.
---@param id string
---@param sprite integer
function functions.SetSprite(id, sprite)
    local entry = blips[id]
    if not entry or not DoesBlipExist(entry.handle) then return end
    SetBlipSprite(entry.handle, sprite)
end

---Update the color of an existing blip.
---@param id string
---@param color integer
function functions.SetColor(id, color)
    local entry = blips[id]
    if not entry or not DoesBlipExist(entry.handle) then return end
    SetBlipColour(entry.handle, color)
end

---Update the scale of an existing blip.
---@param id string
---@param scale number
function functions.SetScale(id, scale)
    local entry = blips[id]
    if not entry or not DoesBlipExist(entry.handle) then return end
    SetBlipScale(entry.handle, scale)
end

---Update the alpha (opacity) of an existing blip.
---@param id string
---@param alpha integer 0â€“255
function functions.SetAlpha(id, alpha)
    local entry = blips[id]
    if not entry or not DoesBlipExist(entry.handle) then return end
    SetBlipAlpha(entry.handle, alpha)
end

---Set blip label text.
---@param id string
---@param label string
function functions.SetLabel(id, label)
    local entry = blips[id]
    if not entry or not DoesBlipExist(entry.handle) then return end
    BeginTextCommandSetBlipName('STRING')
    AddTextComponentSubstringPlayerName(label)
    EndTextCommandSetBlipName(entry.handle)
end

---Toggle blip flash on/off.
---@param id string
---@param state boolean
---@param timer? number ms before flash stops (0 or nil = infinite)
function functions.SetFlash(id, state, timer)
    local entry = blips[id]
    if not entry or not DoesBlipExist(entry.handle) then return end
    SetBlipFlashes(entry.handle, state)
    if state and timer and timer > 0 then
        SetBlipFlashTimer(entry.handle, timer)
    end
end

---Toggle short range on an existing blip.
---@param id string
---@param state boolean
function functions.SetShortRange(id, state)
    local entry = blips[id]
    if not entry or not DoesBlipExist(entry.handle) then return end
    SetBlipAsShortRange(entry.handle, state)
end

---Check if a managed blip still exists.
---@param id string
---@return boolean
function functions.Exists(id)
    local entry = blips[id]
    return entry ~= nil and DoesBlipExist(entry.handle)
end

---Get the raw native handle for a managed blip.
---@param id string
---@return integer|nil
function functions.GetHandle(id)
    local entry = blips[id]
    if not entry then return nil end
    return entry.handle
end

---Get all managed blip IDs.
---@return string[]
function functions.GetAll()
    local ids = {}
    for id in pairs(blips) do
        ids[#ids + 1] = id
    end
    return ids
end

-- â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
-- Cleanup on resource stop
-- â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€

AddEventHandler('onResourceStop', function(resource)
    if resource ~= GetCurrentResourceName() then return end
    functions.RemoveAll()
end)

return functions


########## FILE: \server\commands.lua
-- Rekonstruksi sm_core/server/commands.lua
-- Daftar command mengikuti locale/en.lua (command.*). Memakai lib.addCommand dari ox_lib.
-- Izin admin lewat ACE group (lihat server.cfg: group.god > group.admin > group.mod).

QBCore.Commands = QBCore.Commands or {}

local ADMIN = 'group.admin'
local notify = function(src, text, kind) QBCore.Functions.Notify(src, text, kind or 'inform') end

---Shim API lama qb-core: QBCore.Commands.Add(name, help, arguments, argsrequired, callback, permission)
function QBCore.Commands.Add(name, help, arguments, argsrequired, callback, permission)
    local params = {}
    for i = 1, #(arguments or {}) do
        local arg = arguments[i]
        params[i] = { name = arg.name, help = arg.help, type = 'string', optional = not argsrequired }
    end

    local restricted = false
    if permission and permission ~= 'user' then restricted = 'group.' .. permission end

    lib.addCommand(name, { help = help, params = params, restricted = restricted }, function(source, args, raw)
        local list = {}
        for i = 1, #params do list[#list + 1] = args[params[i].name] end
        callback(source, list, raw)
    end)
end

function QBCore.Commands.Refresh() end -- suggestion sudah ditangani ox_lib

---------------------------------------------------------------- helpers
local function hasPlayerInside(vehicle)
    for seat = -1, 6 do
        local ped = GetPedInVehicleSeat(vehicle, seat)
        if ped and ped ~= 0 and IsPedAPlayer(ped) then return true end
    end
    return false
end

local function getPlayer(src, id)
    local player = QBCore.Functions.GetPlayer(id)
    if not player then notify(src, Lang:t('error.not_online'), 'error') end
    return player
end

---------------------------------------------------------------- teleport
lib.addCommand('tp', {
    help = Lang:t('command.tp.help'),
    params = {
        { name = 'x', type = 'string', help = Lang:t('command.tp.params.x.help'), optional = true },
        { name = 'y', type = 'number', help = Lang:t('command.tp.params.y.help'), optional = true },
        { name = 'z', type = 'number', help = Lang:t('command.tp.params.z.help'), optional = true },
    },
    restricted = ADMIN,
}, function(source, args)
    if args.x and not args.y then
        local target = tonumber(args.x)
        if not target or not GetPlayerName(tostring(target)) then
            return notify(source, Lang:t('error.not_online'), 'error')
        end
        local coords = GetEntityCoords(GetPlayerPed(tostring(target)))
        TriggerClientEvent('qb_event_client:tptocoords', source, { coords.x, coords.y, coords.z })
    elseif args.x and args.y and args.z then
        local x = tonumber(args.x)
        if not x then return notify(source, Lang:t('error.wrong_format'), 'error') end
        TriggerClientEvent('qb_event_client:tptocoords', source, { x, args.y, args.z })
    else
        notify(source, Lang:t('error.missing_args'), 'error')
    end
end)

lib.addCommand('closeserver', {
    help = Lang:t('command.closeserver.help'),
    params = { { name = 'reason', type = 'longString', help = Lang:t('command.closeserver.params.reason.help'), optional = true } },
    restricted = ADMIN,
}, function(source, args)
    if QBConfig.Server.Closed then
        return notify(source, Lang:t('error.server_already_closed'), 'error')
    end

    local reason = args.reason or 'Server Closed'
    QBConfig.Server.Closed = true
    QBConfig.Server.ClosedReason = reason

    for _, id in ipairs(GetPlayers()) do
        if not IsPlayerAceAllowed(id, 'qbadmin.join') then
            DropPlayer(id, reason)
        end
    end
    notify(source, Lang:t('success.server_closed'), 'success')
end)

end)

lib.addCommand('setgang', {
    help = Lang:t('command.setgang.help'),
    params = {
        { name = 'id', type = 'playerId', help = Lang:t('command.setgang.params.id.help') },
        { name = 'gang', type = 'string', help = Lang:t('command.setgang.params.gang.help') },
        { name = 'grade', type = 'number', help = Lang:t('command.setgang.params.grade.help'), optional = true },
    },
    restricted = ADMIN,
}, function(source, args)
    local player = getPlayer(source, args.id)
    if not player then return end
    if not player.Functions.SetGang(args.gang, args.grade or 0) then
        notify(source, Lang:t('error.wrong_format'), 'error')
    end
end)

---------------------------------------------------------------- chat
lib.addCommand('ooc', {
    help = Lang:t('command.ooc.help'),
    params = { { name = 'message', type = 'longString' } },
}, function(source, args)
    TriggerClientEvent('chat:addMessage', -1, {
        color = QBConfig.Commands.OOCColor,
        multiline = true,
        args = { 'OOC | ' .. GetPlayerName(tostring(source)), args.message },
    })
end)

lib.addCommand('me', {
    help = Lang:t('command.me.help'),
    params = { { name = 'message', type = 'longString', help = Lang:t('command.me.params.message.help') } },
}, function(source, args)
    local origin = GetEntityCoords(GetPlayerPed(tostring(source)))
    local name = GetPlayerName(tostring(source))

    for _, id in ipairs(GetPlayers()) do
        local coords = GetEntityCoords(GetPlayerPed(id))
        if #(coords - origin) <= 15.0 then
            TriggerClientEvent('chat:addMessage', tonumber(id), {
                color = { 200, 160, 255 }, multiline = true, args = { name, args.message },
            })
        end
    end
end)


########## FILE: \server\connecting.lua
-- Rekonstruksi sm_core/server/connecting.lua

local function nowUnix() return os.time() end

local function checkBan(src)
    local license = PlayerIdentifier(src, 'license')
    local alt = PlayerIdentifier(src, QBConfig.LicenseType)
    local discord = PlayerIdentifier(src, 'discord')
    local ip = PlayerIdentifier(src, 'ip')

    local row = db.single_async(
        'SELECT id, reason, expire FROM bans WHERE license = ? OR license = ? OR discord = ? OR ip = ? ORDER BY expire DESC LIMIT 1',
        { license or '', alt or '', discord or '', ip or '' })
    if not row then return end

    local expire = tonumber(row.expire) or 0
    if expire > 2147483646 or expire > nowUnix() then
        local remaining = expire > 2147483646 and 'permanen' or os.date('%d-%m-%Y %H:%M', expire)
        return ('Kamu dibanned.\nAlasan: %s\nBerakhir: %s\nDiscord: %s'):format(
            row.reason or '-', remaining, QBConfig.Server.Discord)
    end

    db.update_async('DELETE FROM bans WHERE id = ?', { row.id }) -- ban kedaluwarsa
end

local function onPlayerConnecting(name, setKickReason, deferrals)
    local src = source
    deferrals.defer()
    Wait(0) -- wajib sebelum deferrals.update/done

    if QBConfig.Server.Closed and not IsPlayerAceAllowed(tostring(src), 'qbadmin.join') then
        return deferrals.done(QBConfig.Server.ClosedReason)
    end

    local license = PlayerIdentifier(src, QBConfig.LicenseType)
    if not license then
        if QBConfig.LicenseType == 'steam' then
            return deferrals.done('Steam tidak terdeteksi. Buka Steam, lalu jalankan ulang FiveM.')
        end
        return deferrals.done(Lang:t('error.no_valid_license'))
    end

    if QBConfig.Server.CheckDuplicateLicense and QBCore.Functions.IsLicenseInUse(license) then
        return deferrals.done(Lang:t('error.duplicate_license'))
    end

    deferrals.update(Lang:t('info.checking_ban'):format(name))
    local ok, banReason = pcall(checkBan, src)
    if not ok then
        lib.print.error(banReason)
        return deferrals.done(Lang:t('error.connecting_database_error'))
    end
    if banReason then return deferrals.done(banReason) end

    if QBConfig.Server.Whitelist then
        deferrals.update(Lang:t('info.checking_whitelisted'):format(name))
        if not QBCore.Functions.IsWhitelisted(src) then
            return deferrals.done(Lang:t('error.not_whitelisted'))
        end
    end

    deferrals.done()
end

AddEventHandler('playerConnecting', onPlayerConnecting)


########## FILE: \server\debug.lua
-- Rekonstruksi sm_core/server/debug.lua

local function dump(obj, indent)
    indent = indent or 0
    if type(obj) ~= 'table' then return tostring(obj) end
    local pad = ('  '):rep(indent)
    local out = '{\n'
    for k, v in pairs(obj) do
        out = out .. ('%s  %s = %s\n'):format(pad, tostring(k), type(v) == 'table' and dump(v, indent + 1) or tostring(v))
    end
    return out .. pad .. '}'
end

function QBCore.Debug(resource, obj)
    print(('^3[DEBUG:%s]^7 %s'):format(resource or 'unknown', dump(obj)))
end

function QBCore.ShowError(resource, msg)
    print(('^1[ERROR:%s]^7 %s'):format(resource or 'unknown', msg))
end

function QBCore.ShowSuccess(resource, msg)
    print(('^2[OK:%s]^7 %s'):format(resource or 'unknown', msg))
end

AddEventHandler('QBCore:DebugSomething', function(resource, obj)
    QBCore.Debug(resource, obj)
end)

local function GetQBPlayers()
    return QBCore.Players
end

exports('GetQBPlayers', GetQBPlayers)


########## FILE: \server\events.lua
-- Rekonstruksi sm_core/server/events.lua

AddEventHandler('playerDropped', function()
    local src = source
    local player = QBCore.Players[src]
    if not player then return end

    player.Functions.Save()
    TriggerEvent('QBCore:Server:OnPlayerUnload', src)

    QBCore.Player_Buckets[src] = nil
    QBCore.Players[src] = nil
end)

-- Toggle duty dari client (misal lewat menu/radial)
RegisterNetEvent('QBCore:ToggleDuty', function()
    local src = source
    local player = QBCore.Functions.GetPlayer(src)
    if not player then return end

    local onDuty = not player.PlayerData.job.onduty
    player.Functions.SetJobDuty(onDuty)
    QBCore.Functions.Notify(src, Lang:t(onDuty and 'info.on_duty' or 'info.off_duty'), onDuty and 'success' or 'inform')
end)

-- Client hanya boleh mengubah status kebutuhan dasar (hunger/thirst/stress).
-- Versi qb-core asli mengizinkan metadata apa pun dari client; itu celah, jadi dibatasi di sini.
local clientMeta = { hunger = true, thirst = true, stress = true }

RegisterNetEvent('QBCore:Server:SetMetaData', function(meta, data)
    local src = source
    local player = QBCore.Functions.GetPlayer(src)
    if not player then return end
    if not clientMeta[meta] or type(data) ~= 'number' then return end

    player.Functions.SetMetaData(meta, data)

    if meta == 'hunger' or meta == 'thirst' then
        TriggerClientEvent('hud:client:UpdateNeeds', src,
            player.PlayerData.metadata.hunger, player.PlayerData.metadata.thirst)
    end
end)

-- Resource lain (mis. sm_character) sering memanggil ini setelah karakter dipilih.
-- Hanya diteruskan sebagai event server; core tidak melakukan apa-apa selain broadcast.
RegisterNetEvent('QBCore:Server:OnPlayerLoaded', function()
    local src = source
    if not QBCore.Players[src] then return end
    TriggerEvent('QBCore:Server:PlayerLoadedFromClient', src)
end)


########## FILE: \server\functions.lua
-- sm_core/server/functions.lua
-- API QBCore.Functions yang dipanggil commands/loops/connecting tetapi belum pernah didefinisikan.
-- Kompatibel qb-core; semua akses lewat QBCore.Players, tidak membuat tabel baru.

QBCore.Functions = QBCore.Functions or {}

---@param source Source|string|number
---@return table?
function QBCore.Functions.GetPlayer(source)
    local src = tonumber(source)
    if not src then return end
    return QBCore.Players[src]
end

---@return table
function QBCore.Functions.GetPlayers()
    return QBCore.Players
end

---@param citizenid string
---@return table?
function QBCore.Functions.GetPlayerByCitizenId(citizenid)
    for _, player in pairs(QBCore.Players) do
        if player.PlayerData.citizenid == citizenid then return player end
    end
end

---@param phone string
---@return table?
function QBCore.Functions.GetPlayerByPhone(phone)
    for _, player in pairs(QBCore.Players) do
        if player.PlayerData.charinfo.phone == phone then return player end
    end
end

---@param job string
---@return table[]
function QBCore.Functions.GetPlayersByJob(job)
    local list = {}
    for _, player in pairs(QBCore.Players) do
        if player.PlayerData.job.name == job then list[#list + 1] = player end
    end
    return list
end

---@param gang string
---@return table[]
function QBCore.Functions.GetPlayersByGang(gang)
    local list = {}
    for _, player in pairs(QBCore.Players) do
        if player.PlayerData.gang.name == gang then list[#list + 1] = player end
    end
    return list
end

---@param identifier string
---@return number?
function QBCore.Functions.GetSource(identifier)
    for src, player in pairs(QBCore.Players) do
        if player.PlayerData.license == identifier then return src end
    end
end

---@param source Source|string|number
---@param idtype? string
---@return string?
function QBCore.Functions.GetIdentifier(source, idtype)
    return PlayerIdentifier(source, idtype)
end

---@param source Source|string|number
---@return string[]
function QBCore.Functions.GetPlayerIdentifiers(source)
    return GetPlayerIdentifiers(tostring(source))
end

---@param license string
---@return boolean
function QBCore.Functions.IsLicenseInUse(license)
    for _, player in pairs(QBCore.Players) do
        if player.PlayerData.license == license then return true end
    end
    return false
end

---@param source Source|string|number
---@return boolean
function QBCore.Functions.IsWhitelisted(source)
    local src = tostring(source)

    if IsPlayerAceAllowed(src, QBConfig.Server.WhitelistPermission) then return true end

    for i = 1, #QBConfig.Server.Permissions do
        if IsPlayerAceAllowed(src, QBConfig.Server.Permissions[i]) then return true end
    end

    return false
end

---@param source Source|string|number
---@param text string
---@param kind? string
---@param duration? number
function QBCore.Functions.Notify(source, text, kind, duration)
    TriggerClientEvent('ox_lib:notify', tonumber(source) or source, {
        description = text,
        type = kind or 'inform',
        duration = duration,
    })
end

---@param source Source|string|number
---@param permission string
function QBCore.Functions.AddPermission(source, permission)
    local player = QBCore.Functions.GetPlayer(source)
    if not player then return end

    ExecuteCommand(('add_principal identifier.%s group.%s'):format(player.PlayerData.license, permission))
end

---@param source Source|string|number
---@param permission string
function QBCore.Functions.RemovePermission(source, permission)
    local player = QBCore.Functions.GetPlayer(source)
    if not player then return end

    ExecuteCommand(('remove_principal identifier.%s group.%s'):format(player.PlayerData.license, permission))
end

---@param name string
---@param cb fun(source: number, ...): any
function QBCore.Functions.CreateCallback(name, cb)
    lib.callback.register(name, cb)
end

########## FILE: \server\loops.lua
-- Rekonstruksi sm_core/server/loops.lua

-- Simpan berkala + penurunan hunger/thirst.
-- Siklus biasa  : SaveLight (money, position, metadata)
-- Tiap FullSaveEvery siklus: Save penuh (semua kolom kecuali inventory)
CreateThread(function()
    local cycle = 0
    while true do
        Wait(QBConfig.UpdateInterval * 60000)
        cycle += 1
        local full = cycle % QBConfig.FullSaveEvery == 0

        local sources = {}
        for src in pairs(QBCore.Players) do sources[#sources + 1] = src end

        for i = 1, #sources do
            local src = sources[i]
            local player = QBCore.Players[src]
            if player then
                local meta = player.PlayerData.metadata
                if not meta.isdead then
                    player.Functions.SetMetaData('thirst', math.max(0, (meta.thirst or 100) - QBConfig.Player.ThirstRate))
                    player.Functions.SetMetaData('hunger', math.max(0, (meta.hunger or 100) - QBConfig.Player.HungerRate))
                    TriggerClientEvent('hud:client:UpdateNeeds', src, meta.hunger, meta.thirst)
                end
                if full then QBCore.Player.Save(src) else QBCore.Player.SaveLight(src) end
                Wait(10)
            end
        end
    end
end)

-- Gaji berkala
CreateThread(function()
    if QBConfig.Money.PayCheckSociety then
        lib.print.warn('PayCheckSociety=true belum didukung di rekonstruksi ini; gaji dibayar langsung ke bank.')
    end

    while true do
        Wait(QBConfig.Money.PayCheckTimeOut * 60000)

        for src, player in pairs(QBCore.Players) do
            local job = player.PlayerData.job
            local shared = job and QBShared.Jobs[job.name]
            local payment = job and job.payment or 0

            if shared and payment > 0 and (job.onduty or shared.offDutyPay) then
                if player.Functions.AddMoney('bank', payment, 'paycheck') then
                    QBCore.Functions.Notify(src, Lang:t('info.received_paycheck', { value = payment }), 'success')
                end
            end
        end
    end
end)


########## FILE: \server\player\identifier.lua
---Returns the identifier (with prefix, e.g. 'license:abc' or 'steam:110000...') of a connected player.
---`idtype` defaults to 'license' (qb-core behaviour). Core code passes QBConfig.LicenseType explicitly.
---@param source Source|string
---@param idtype? string
---@return string?
function PlayerIdentifier(source, idtype)
    return GetPlayerIdentifierByType(tostring(source), idtype or 'license')
end


########## FILE: \server\player\main.lua
-- Rekonstruksi sm_core/server/player/main.lua (qb-core compatible)
-- Inventory TIDAK disimpan di sini: kolom `players.inventory` dikelola ox_inventory.

QBCore.Players = QBCore.Players or {}
QBCore.Player = QBCore.Player or {}

-- Tabel lain yang berisi citizenid dan ikut terhapus saat karakter dihapus.
-- Tambahkan sesuai hasil analisis resource sm_* (format: tabel = kolom).
QBCore.Player.CitizenTables = { players = 'citizenid' }

---------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- helpers
---------------------------------------------------------------------------

local function resolve(value)
    local kind = type(value)
    if kind == 'function' then return value() end
    if kind == 'table' then
        local copy = {}
        for k, v in pairs(value) do copy[k] = resolve(v) end
        return copy
    end
    return value
end

local function fillDefaults(data, defaults)
    for key, default in pairs(defaults) do
        local current = data[key]
        if current == nil then
            data[key] = resolve(default)
        elseif type(current) == 'table' and type(default) == 'table' then
            fillDefaults(current, default)
        end
    end
    return data
end

local function toVec4(pos)
    local kind = type(pos)
    if kind == 'vector4' then return pos end
    if kind == 'vector3' then return vec4(pos.x, pos.y, pos.z, 0.0) end
    if kind == 'table' and pos.x and pos.y and pos.z then
        return vec4(pos.x + 0.0, pos.y + 0.0, pos.z + 0.0, (pos.w or pos.h or 0.0) + 0.0)
    end
    return QBConfig.DefaultSpawn
end

local function safeDecode(raw, fallback)
    if type(raw) ~= 'string' or raw == '' then return fallback end
    local ok, decoded = pcall(json.decode, raw)
    if ok and decoded ~= nil then return decoded end
    return fallback
end

local function uniqueMeta(path, generate)
    local value
    repeat
        value = generate()
        local taken = db.scalar_async(
            "SELECT 1 FROM players WHERE JSON_UNQUOTE(JSON_EXTRACT(metadata, '" .. path .. "')) = ? LIMIT 1",
            { value })
    until not taken
    return value
end

---------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- id generators (dipakai oleh config.lua -> PlayerDefaults)
---------------------------------------------------------------------------

function QBCore.Player.CreateCitizenId()
    local id
    repeat
        id = lib.string.random('AAA11111')
        local taken = db.scalar_async('SELECT 1 FROM players WHERE citizenid = ? LIMIT 1', { id })
    until not taken
    return id
end

function QBCore.Player.CreateFingerId()
    return uniqueMeta('$.fingerprint', function() return lib.string.random('Aa111AAa11AAA1111') end)
end

function QBCore.Player.CreateWalletId()
    return uniqueMeta('$.walletid', function() return 'SM-' .. math.random(11111111, 99999999) end)
end

function QBCore.Player.CreateSerialNumber()
    return uniqueMeta('$.phonedata.SerialNumber', function() return tostring(math.random(11111111, 99999999)) end)
end

---------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- job / gang builders (dibaca ulang dari QBShared supaya selalu sinkron)
---------------------------------------------------------------------------

---@return table?
function QBCore.Player.BuildJob(name, grade, onDuty)
    local job = QBShared.Jobs[name]
    if not job then return end
    local gradeKey = tostring(grade or 0)
    local gradeData = job.grades[gradeKey]
    if not gradeData then return end

    if onDuty == nil then onDuty = job.defaultDuty end

    return {
        name = name,
        label = job.label,
        payment = gradeData.payment or 30,
        type = job.type or 'none',
        onduty = onDuty and true or false,
        isboss = gradeData.isboss or false,
        grade = { name = gradeData.name, level = tonumber(gradeKey) },
    }
end

---@return table?
function QBCore.Player.BuildGang(name, grade)
    local gang = QBShared.Gangs[name]
    if not gang then return end
    local gradeKey = tostring(grade or 0)
    local gradeData = gang.grades[gradeKey]
    if not gradeData then return end

    return {
        name = name,
        label = gang.label,
        isboss = gradeData.isboss or false,
        grade = { name = gradeData.name, level = tonumber(gradeKey) },
    }
end

---------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- player data
---------------------------------------------------------------------------

local function decodeRow(row)
    return {
        citizenid = row.citizenid,
        cid = tonumber(row.cid) or 1,
        license = row.license,
        name = row.name,
        money = safeDecode(row.money, {}),
        charinfo = safeDecode(row.charinfo, {}),
        job = safeDecode(row.job, {}),
        gang = safeDecode(row.gang, {}),
        position = toVec4(safeDecode(row.position, nil)),
        metadata = safeDecode(row.metadata, {}),
        items = {},
    }
end

---@param source? Source nil => offline
---@param PlayerData? table
function QBCore.Player.CheckPlayerData(source, PlayerData)
    PlayerData = PlayerData or {}
    local offline = true

    if source then
        PlayerData.source = source
        PlayerData.license = PlayerData.license or QBCore.Functions.GetIdentifier(source, QBConfig.LicenseType)
        PlayerData.name = GetPlayerName(tostring(source))
        offline = false
    end

    -- money: pastikan semua tipe uang ada
    PlayerData.money = type(PlayerData.money) == 'table' and PlayerData.money or {}
    for moneytype, start in pairs(QBConfig.Money.MoneyTypes) do
        if PlayerData.money[moneytype] == nil then PlayerData.money[moneytype] = start end
    end

    fillDefaults(PlayerData, QBConfig.Player.PlayerDefaults)

    -- job / gang divalidasi terhadap QBShared (kalau sudah dihapus => reset)
    local job = PlayerData.job
    local builtJob = job and job.name and QBCore.Player.BuildJob(job.name, job.grade and job.grade.level, job.onduty)
    if builtJob then
        if QBShared.ForceJobDefaultDutyAtLogin and not offline then
            builtJob.onduty = QBShared.Jobs[builtJob.name].defaultDuty and true or false
        end
        PlayerData.job = builtJob
    else
        PlayerData.job = QBCore.Player.BuildJob('unemployed', 0)
    end

    local gang = PlayerData.gang
    local builtGang = gang and gang.name and QBCore.Player.BuildGang(gang.name, gang.grade and gang.grade.level)
    PlayerData.gang = builtGang or QBCore.Player.BuildGang('none', 0)

    PlayerData.position = toVec4(PlayerData.position)
    PlayerData.items = PlayerData.items or {}

    return QBCore.Player.CreatePlayer(PlayerData, offline)
end

function QBCore.Player.CreatePlayer(PlayerData, Offline)
    local self = { Functions = {}, PlayerData = PlayerData, Offline = Offline }

    SetupPlayerMethods(self) -- server/player/method.lua

    if self.Offline then return self end

    local src = self.PlayerData.source
    QBCore.Players[src] = self
    Player(src).state:set('isLoggedIn', true, true)
    QBCore.Player.Save(src)

    -- instance siap dipakai resource lain (ox_inventory bridge mendengarkan event ini)
    TriggerEvent('QBCore:Server:PlayerLoaded', self)
    self.Functions.UpdatePlayerData()

    return self
end

---@param source Source|string|number
---@param key? string
function QBCore.Player.GetPlayerData(source, key)
    local player = QBCore.Players[tonumber(source)]
    if not player then return end
    if key then return player.PlayerData[key] end
    return player.PlayerData
end

function QBCore.Player.GetOfflinePlayer(citizenid)
    if not citizenid then return end
    local row = db.single_async('SELECT * FROM players WHERE citizenid = ?', { citizenid })
    if not row then return end
    return QBCore.Player.CheckPlayerData(nil, decodeRow(row))
end

---@return boolean
function QBCore.Player.Login(source, citizenid, newData)
    if not source or source == '' then return false end

    if citizenid then
        local license = QBCore.Functions.GetIdentifier(source, QBConfig.LicenseType)
        local row = db.single_async('SELECT * FROM players WHERE citizenid = ?', { citizenid })
        if row and license and row.license == license then
            QBCore.Player.CheckPlayerData(source, decodeRow(row))
            return true
        end
        DropPlayer(tostring(source), Lang:t('info.exploit_dropped'))
        return false
    end

    QBCore.Player.CheckPlayerData(source, newData)
    return true
end

---------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- saving
---------------------------------------------------------------------------

local SAVE_QUERY = [[
    INSERT INTO players (citizenid, cid, license, name, money, charinfo, job, gang, position, metadata)
    VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
    ON DUPLICATE KEY UPDATE
        name = VALUES(name), money = VALUES(money), charinfo = VALUES(charinfo),
        job = VALUES(job), gang = VALUES(gang), position = VALUES(position), metadata = VALUES(metadata)
]]

local LIGHT_QUERY = 'UPDATE players SET money = ?, position = ?, metadata = ? WHERE citizenid = ?'

local function syncFromPed(player)
    local data = player.PlayerData
    local ped = GetPlayerPed(tostring(data.source))
    if not ped or ped == 0 or not DoesEntityExist(ped) then return end

    local coords = GetEntityCoords(ped)
    if coords.x ~= 0.0 or coords.y ~= 0.0 then
        data.position = vec4(coords.x, coords.y, coords.z, GetEntityHeading(ped))
    end

    if not data.metadata.isdead and not data.metadata.inlaststand then
        local health = GetEntityHealth(ped)
        if health and health > 100 then data.metadata.health = health end
        data.metadata.armor = GetPedArmour(ped)
    end
end

local function fullParams(data)
    return {
        data.citizenid, data.cid, data.license, data.name,
        json.encode(data.money), json.encode(data.charinfo), json.encode(data.job), json.encode(data.gang),
        json.encode(data.position), json.encode(data.metadata),
    }
end

---Simpan semua kolom (kecuali inventory).
function QBCore.Player.Save(source)
    local player = QBCore.Players[tonumber(source)]
    if not player then return false end
    syncFromPed(player)
    db.insert_async(SAVE_QUERY, fullParams(player.PlayerData))
    return true
end

---Simpan ringan (money + position + metadata). Dipakai di siklus QBConfig.UpdateInterval biasa.
function QBCore.Player.SaveLight(source)
    local player = QBCore.Players[tonumber(source)]
    if not player then return false end
    syncFromPed(player)
    local data = player.PlayerData
    db.update_async(LIGHT_QUERY, {
        json.encode(data.money), json.encode(data.position), json.encode(data.metadata), data.citizenid,
    })
    return true
end

function QBCore.Player.SaveOffline(PlayerData)
    if not PlayerData then return false end
    db.insert_async(SAVE_QUERY, fullParams(PlayerData))
    return true
end

---------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- logout / delete
---------------------------------------------------------------------------

function QBCore.Player.Logout(source)
    source = tonumber(source)
    local player = QBCore.Players[source]
    if not player then return end

    QBCore.Player.Save(source)

    TriggerClientEvent('QBCore:Client:OnPlayerUnload', source)
    TriggerEvent('QBCore:Server:OnPlayerUnload', source)
    TriggerClientEvent('QBCore:Player:setPData', source, 'all', {})
    Player(source).state:set('isLoggedIn', false, true)

    Wait(200)
    QBCore.Players[source] = nil
end

function QBCore.Player.ForceDeleteCharacter(citizenid)
    local online = QBCore.Functions.GetPlayerByCitizenId(citizenid)
    if online then
        DropPlayer(tostring(online.PlayerData.source), 'Karakter kamu dihapus oleh server.')
    end

    for tableName, column in pairs(QBCore.Player.CitizenTables) do
        local ok, err = pcall(function()
            db.update_async(('DELETE FROM `%s` WHERE `%s` = ?'):format(tableName, column), { citizenid })
        end)
        if not ok then lib.print.warn(('delete %s.%s gagal: %s'):format(tableName, column, err)) end
    end

    TriggerEvent('QBCore:Server:OnPlayerDeleted', citizenid)
    return true
end

function QBCore.Player.DeleteCharacter(source, citizenid)
    local license = QBCore.Functions.GetIdentifier(source, QBConfig.LicenseType)
    local ownerLicense = db.scalar_async('SELECT license FROM players WHERE citizenid = ?', { citizenid })

    if ownerLicense and license and ownerLicense == license then
        return QBCore.Player.ForceDeleteCharacter(citizenid)
    end

    DropPlayer(tostring(source), Lang:t('info.exploit_dropped'))
    return false
end


########## FILE: \server\player\method.lua
-- Rekonstruksi sm_core/server/player/method.lua (qb-core compatible)

local function clamp(value, min, max)
    return math.max(min, math.min(max, value))
end

---Wrapper yang aman dikirim lintas resource (fungsi jadi function reference).
---@param player table
---@return table
function PreparePlayerTable(player)
    return { PlayerData = player.PlayerData, Functions = player.Functions, Offline = player.Offline }
end

---@param self table
function SetupPlayerMethods(self)
    local fn = self.Functions

    -- kirim satu key (atau semua) ke client. Client: event QBCore:Player:setPData
    local function sync(key, val, subVal)
        if self.Offline then return end
        local src = self.PlayerData.source
        if key == nil then
            TriggerEvent('QBCore:Player:SetPlayerData', self.PlayerData)
            TriggerClientEvent('QBCore:Player:setPData', src, 'all', self.PlayerData)
        elseif subVal ~= nil then
            TriggerClientEvent('QBCore:Player:setPData', src, key, val, subVal)
        else
            TriggerClientEvent('QBCore:Player:setPData', src, key, val)
        end
    end

    function fn.UpdatePlayerData()
        sync()
    end

    function fn.SetPlayerData(key, val)
        if type(key) ~= 'string' then return end
        self.PlayerData[key] = val
        sync(key, val)
    end

    ---------------------------------------------------------------- metadata
    function fn.SetMetaData(meta, val)
        if type(meta) ~= 'string' then return end

        if (meta == 'hunger' or meta == 'thirst' or meta == 'stress') and type(val) == 'number' then
            val = clamp(val, 0, 100)
        end

        self.PlayerData.metadata[meta] = val

        if val == nil then
            sync('metadata', self.PlayerData.metadata)
        else
            sync('metadata', meta, val)
        end

        if meta == 'inlaststand' or meta == 'isdead' then
            fn.Save()
        end
    end

    function fn.GetMetaData(meta)
        if meta == nil then return self.PlayerData.metadata end
        return self.PlayerData.metadata[meta]
    end

    ---------------------------------------------------------------- job / gang
    function fn.SetJob(job, grade)
        if not job then return false end
        local built = QBCore.Player.BuildJob(tostring(job), grade or 0)
        if not built then return false end

        self.PlayerData.job = built
        if not self.Offline then
            local src = self.PlayerData.source
            sync('job', built)
            TriggerEvent('QBCore:Server:OnJobUpdate', src, built)
            TriggerClientEvent('QBCore:Client:OnJobUpdate', src, built)
        end
        return true
    end

    function fn.SetGang(gang, grade)
        if not gang then return false end
        local built = QBCore.Player.BuildGang(tostring(gang), grade or 0)
        if not built then return false end

        self.PlayerData.gang = built
        if not self.Offline then
            local src = self.PlayerData.source
            sync('gang', built)
            TriggerEvent('QBCore:Server:OnGangUpdate', src, built)
            TriggerClientEvent('QBCore:Client:OnGangUpdate', src, built)
        end
        return true
    end

    function fn.SetJobDuty(onDuty)
        self.PlayerData.job.onduty = onDuty and true or false
        if not self.Offline then
            local src = self.PlayerData.source
            sync('job', self.PlayerData.job)
            TriggerEvent('QBCore:Server:SetDuty', src, self.PlayerData.job.onduty)
            TriggerClientEvent('QBCore:Client:SetDuty', src, self.PlayerData.job.onduty)
        end
    end

    ---------------------------------------------------------------- money
    local function moneyEvents(moneytype, amount, action, reason, hudNegative)
        if self.Offline then return end
        local src = self.PlayerData.source
        sync('money', self.PlayerData.money)
        TriggerClientEvent('hud:client:OnMoneyChange', src, moneytype, amount, hudNegative)
        TriggerClientEvent('QBCore:Client:OnMoneyChange', src, moneytype, amount, action, reason)
        TriggerEvent('QBCore:Server:OnMoneyChange', src, moneytype, amount, action, reason)
    end

    function fn.GetMoney(moneytype)
        moneytype = tostring(moneytype or ''):lower()
        return self.PlayerData.money[moneytype] or false
    end

    function fn.AddMoney(moneytype, amount, reason)
        reason = reason or 'unknown'
        moneytype = tostring(moneytype or ''):lower()
        amount = tonumber(amount)
        if not amount or amount < 0 then return false end
        if self.PlayerData.money[moneytype] == nil then return false end

        self.PlayerData.money[moneytype] += amount
        moneyEvents(moneytype, amount, 'add', reason, false)
        return true
    end

    function fn.RemoveMoney(moneytype, amount, reason)
        reason = reason or 'unknown'
        moneytype = tostring(moneytype or ''):lower()
        amount = tonumber(amount)
        if not amount or amount < 0 then return false end
        local balance = self.PlayerData.money[moneytype]
        if balance == nil then return false end

        for _, noMinus in pairs(QBConfig.Money.DontAllowMinus) do
            if noMinus == moneytype and balance - amount < 0 then return false end
        end
        if balance - amount < QBConfig.Money.MinusLimit then return false end

        self.PlayerData.money[moneytype] = balance - amount
        moneyEvents(moneytype, amount, 'remove', reason, true)
        return true
    end

    function fn.SetMoney(moneytype, amount, reason)
        reason = reason or 'unknown'
        moneytype = tostring(moneytype or ''):lower()
        amount = tonumber(amount)
        if not amount or amount < 0 then return false end
        local old = self.PlayerData.money[moneytype]
        if old == nil then return false end

        self.PlayerData.money[moneytype] = amount
        local difference = amount - old
        moneyEvents(moneytype, math.abs(difference), 'set', reason, difference < 0)
        return true
    end

    ---------------------------------------------------------------- inventory (ox_inventory)
    -- Pemetaan ini ASUMSI standar ox_inventory; sesuaikan jika resource sm_* memakai API lain.
    function fn.AddItem(item, amount, slot, info)
        return exports.ox_inventory:AddItem(self.PlayerData.source, item, amount or 1, info, slot)
    end

    function fn.RemoveItem(item, amount, slot, info)
        return exports.ox_inventory:RemoveItem(self.PlayerData.source, item, amount or 1, info, slot)
    end

    function fn.GetItemByName(item)
        return exports.ox_inventory:GetItem(self.PlayerData.source, item, nil, false)
    end

    function fn.GetItemsByName(item)
        return exports.ox_inventory:Search(self.PlayerData.source, 'slots', item)
    end

    function fn.ClearInventory(keep)
        return exports.ox_inventory:ClearInventory(self.PlayerData.source, keep)
    end

    ---------------------------------------------------------------- lifecycle
    function fn.Save()
        if self.Offline then
            QBCore.Player.SaveOffline(self.PlayerData)
        else
            QBCore.Player.Save(self.PlayerData.source)
        end
    end

    function fn.Logout()
        if self.Offline then return end
        QBCore.Player.Logout(self.PlayerData.source)
    end

    function fn.AddMethod(methodName, handler)
        fn[methodName] = handler
    end

    function fn.AddField(fieldName, data)
        self[fieldName] = data
    end
end

########## FILE: \shared\locale.lua
--- @class Locale
Locale = {}
Locale.__index = Locale

local function translateKey(phrase, subs)
    if type(phrase) ~= 'string' then
        error('TypeError: translateKey function expects arg #1 to be a string')
    end

    -- Substituions
    if not subs then
        return phrase
    end

    -- We should be escaping gsub just in case of any
    -- shenanigans with nested template patterns or injection

    -- Create and copy our return string
    local result = phrase

    -- Initial Scan over result looking for substituions
    for k, v in pairs(subs) do
        local templateToFind = '%%{' .. k .. '}'
        result = result:gsub(templateToFind, tostring(v)) -- string to allow all types
    end

    return result
end

--- Constructor function for a new Locale class instance
--- @param opts table<string, any> - Constructor opts param
--- @return Locale
function Locale.new(_, opts)
    local self = setmetatable({}, Locale)

    self.fallback = opts.fallbackLang and Locale:new({
        warnOnMissing = false,
        phrases = opts.fallbackLang.phrases,
    }) or false

    self.warnOnMissing = type(opts.warnOnMissing) ~= 'boolean' and true or opts.warnOnMissing

    self.phrases = {}
    self:extend(opts.phrases or {})

    return self
end

--- Method for extending an instances phrases map. This is also, used
--- internally for initial population of phrases field.
--- @param phrases table<string, string> - Table of phrase definitions
--- @param prefix string | nil - Optional prefix used for recursive calls
--- @return nil
function Locale:extend(phrases, prefix)
    for key, phrase in pairs(phrases) do
        local prefixKey = prefix and ('%s.%s'):format(prefix, key) or key
        -- If this is a nested table, we need to go reeeeeeeeeeeecursive
        if type(phrase) == 'table' then
            self:extend(phrase, prefixKey)
        else
            self.phrases[prefixKey] = phrase
        end
    end
end

--- Clear locale instance phrases
--- Might be useful for memory management of large phrase maps.
--- @return nil
function Locale:clear()
    self.phrases = {}
end

--- Clears all phrases and replaces it with the passed phrases table
--- @param phrases table<string, any>
function Locale:replace(phrases)
    phrases = phrases or {}
    self:clear()
    self:extend(phrases)
end

--- Gets & Sets a locale depending on if an argument is passed
--- @param newLocale string - Optional new locale to set
--- @return string
function Locale:locale(newLocale)
    if (newLocale) then
        self.currentLocale = newLocale
    end
    return self.currentLocale
end

--- Primary translation method for a phrase of given key
--- @param key string - The phrase key to target
--- @param subs table<string, any> | nil
--- @return string
function Locale:t(key, subs)
    local phrase, result
    subs = subs or {}

    -- See if the passed key resolves to a valid phrase string
    if type(self.phrases[key]) == 'string' then
        phrase = self.phrases[key]
        -- At this point we know whether the phrase does not exist for this key
    else
        if self.warnOnMissing then
            print(('^3Warning: Missing phrase for key: "%s"^0'):format(key))
        end
        if self.fallback then
            return self.fallback:t(key, subs)
        end
        result = key
    end

    if type(phrase) == 'string' then
        result = translateKey(phrase, subs)
    end

    return result
end

--- Check if a phrase key has already been defined within the Locale instance phrase maps.
--- @return boolean
function Locale:has(key)
    return self.phrases[key] ~= nil
end

--- Will remove phrase keys from a Locale instance, using recursion/
--- @param phraseTarget string | table
--- @param prefix string
function Locale:delete(phraseTarget, prefix)
    -- If the target is a string, we know that this is the end
    -- of nested table tree.
    if type(phraseTarget) == 'string' then
        self.phrases[phraseTarget] = nil
    else
        for key, phrase in pairs(phraseTarget) do
            local prefixKey = prefix and prefix .. '.' .. key or key

            if type(phrase) == 'table' then
                self:delete(phrase, prefixKey)
            else
                self.phrases[prefixKey] = nil
            end
        end
    end
end

---@deprecated use lib.string.random from ox_lib
QBShared.RandomStr = function(length)
    if length <= 0 then return '' end
    local pattern = math.random(2) == 1 and 'a' or 'A'

    ---@diagnostic disable-next-line: deprecated
    return QBShared.RandomStr(length - 1) .. lib.string.random(pattern)
end

---@deprecated use lib.string.random from ox_lib
QBShared.RandomInt = function(length)
    if length <= 0 then return '' end

    ---@diagnostic disable-next-line: deprecated
    return QBShared.RandomInt(length - 1) .. lib.string.random('1')
end

---@deprecated use string.strsplit with CfxLua 5.4
QBShared.SplitStr = function(str, delimiter)
    local result = table.pack(string.strsplit(delimiter, str))
    result.n = nil
    return result
end

---@deprecated use qbLib.string.trim from modules/lib.lua
QBShared.Trim = function(str)
    if not str then return nil end
    return qbLib.string.trim(str)
end

---@deprecated use qbLib.string.capitalize from modules/lib.lua
QBShared.FirstToUpper = function(str)
    if not str then return nil end
    return qbLib.string.capitalize(str)
end

---@deprecated use qbLib.math.round from modules/lib.lua
QBShared.Round = qbLib.math.round

---@deprecated use qbLib.setVehicleExtra from modules/lib.lua
QBShared.ChangeVehicleExtra = qbLib.setVehicleExtras


QBShared.IsFunction = function(value)
    if type(value) == 'table' then
        return value.__cfx_functionReference ~= nil and type(value.__cfx_functionReference) == "string"
    end

    return type(value) == 'function'
end

QBShared.VehicleHashes = QBShared.VehicleHashes or {}
for i = 1, #Vehicles do
    local hash = joaat(Vehicles[i].model)
    QBShared.Vehicles[Vehicles[i].model] = {
        spawncode = Vehicles[i].model,
        name = Vehicles[i].name,
        brand = Vehicles[i].brand,
        model = Vehicles[i].model,
        price = Vehicles[i].price,
        category = Vehicles[i].category,
        hash = hash,
        type = Vehicles[i].type,
        shop = Vehicles[i].shop
    }
    QBShared.VehicleHashes[hash] = QBShared.Vehicles[Vehicles[i].model]
end
